Result: 基准已构建完成，包含大规模多语言文化对话数据集，并确立了三大评估任务，可用于系统性探测大模型在文化接地语言上的理解、迁移与生成表现。

Conclusion: CultureTalk-ID填补了印尼及地方语言对话式文化常识评估的空白，为大模型在真实文化语境下的能力测试提供了更真实、全面的基准。

Abstract: Culture is lived through conversation, yet existing Indonesian cultural commonsense benchmarks evaluate LLMs on short and isolated prompts, stripping away the dialogic context in which cultural nuances actually surface. We introduce CultureTalk-ID, the first dialogue-based benchmark for cultural commonsense in Indonesian and its local languages, comprising 4,496 culturally grounded dialogues across 11 languages and 13 culturally salient topics, curated through a multi-stage human pipeline with native speakers to ensure authenticity. CultureTalk-ID introduces three complementary tasks, namely dialogue-based multiple-choice cultural commonsense reasoning, culturally faithful machine translation, and language steering, which jointly probe whether LLMs can understand, transfer, and generate culturally grounded language.

</details>


### [45] [Sample-Efficient Learning from Agent Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21051)
*Chenhui Gou,Haoqin Tu,Yunhao Fang,Jianfei Cai,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Experience Distillation（经验蒸馏）方法，将智能体的交互历史内化到模型权重中，无需额外环境交互，在软件工程任务和文本冒险游戏上保留了至少64.8%的上下文学习收益，且仅用9.6倍更少的环境样本即可匹配传统强化学习基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的智能体学习受限于昂贵的环境交互成本。上下文学习虽样本高效，但一旦经验移出上下文，收益即消失；上下文蒸馏虽可将上下文信息内化到权重，但将其应用于智能体交互历史且不牺牲环境样本效率尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Experience Distillation实现，仅利用已收集的经验数据，无需额外环境交互，将试错经验通过蒸馏内化到模型权重中。在749个软件工程任务和6个文本冒险游戏上进行实验验证。

Result: 经验蒸馏在两个领域均保留了至少64.8%的上下文学习收益，而直接在收集经验上进行监督微调仅恢复3.8%。与经典强化学习基线相比，结合试错经验的上下文学习后经验蒸馏，仅用至少9.6倍更少的环境样本即可达到相当性能。

Conclusion: 经验蒸馏有效解决了智能体经验内化难题，在保持极高环境样本效率的同时，大幅保留了上下文学习的性能增益，为实际部署中的智能体持续学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Real-world agent learning is often constrained by costly environment interactions, such as running time-consuming experiments or obtaining human feedback. In-context learning offers a highly sample-efficient way for agents to learn from their own interaction histories, but its gains disappear once that experience is removed from the context. Separately, context distillation provides a mechanism for internalizing contextual information into model weights. However, applying it to agents' interaction histories without sacrificing environment sample efficiency remains underexplored. We term this problem Experience Distillation and develop an implementation that requires no further environment interaction beyond the collected experience. Experiments on 749 curated software-engineering tasks and six text-adventure games show that it retains at least 64.8\% of the gains from in-context learning across both domains, whereas direct supervised fine-tuning on the collected experience recovers only 3.8\%. Compared with classical reinforcement-learning baselines, in-context learning from trial-and-error experience followed by Experience Distillation matches their performance with at least \(9.6\times\) fewer environment samples.

</details>


### [46] [QuantiBias: Benchmarking Quantization-Induced Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21063)
*Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 量化后的大语言模型虽然通过标准安全评估（拒答、过拒、选择题偏见），但在开放式生成中显著增加刻板印象输出（约24-27%），提出QuantiBias基准揭示量化将精度分配给无偏见预防信号的能力数据，推理可部分缓解但效果因模型族而异，量化构建必须重新评估开放式偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型发布前普遍经过量化压缩，学界默认该步骤无害且极少重新检查安全性。现有标准安全评估（拒答有害请求、避免过拒、选择题无偏答案）无法捕捉开放式生成中的偏见增加，导致量化模型在通过所有标准检查后仍向用户输出更多刻板印象。

Method: 提出QuantiBias基准：包含生成式多语言刻板印象探针、拒答与选择题控制任务以隔离开放式生成、对比推理前后的输出、对生成内容的严重性评级。在Qwen和Gemma两个主干模型、五个量化族、八个基准上系统评估量化对开放式偏见的影响。

Result: 量化模型在八种语言的开放式回答中约1/4输出刻板印象（24%-27%），通过所有标准检查。量化将额外精度分配给不含偏见预防信号的能力数据。回答前推理在某些模型族上约减半偏见效果，但在其他族无效果。开放式偏见随压缩程度增加的趋势不确定，对评分裁判敏感。

Conclusion: 量化构建必须专门针对开放式偏见重新评估，不能仅依赖其已通过的短形式防护测试。标准安全评估存在盲区，需引入生成式、多语言、含严重性评级的开放式偏见基准（如QuantiBias）进行补充验证。

Abstract: Almost every large language model that reaches a broad audience is quantized: trained in full precision, then compressed for efficiency. This step is assumed harmless and its safety is rarely re-checked. We find its principal side effect is increased bias that standard safety evaluation misses. Holding the model, its training, and the prompts fixed, a quantized model still refuses harmful requests, still avoids over-refusing benign prompts, and still selects the unbiased multiple-choice answer. Yet asked an open-ended question, the same model volunteers stereotypes in all eight languages we probe, in roughly one in four open-ended answers under an independent judge (~24% to ~27% across the compression ladder): it passes every standard check and still reaches users measurably more biased. The selective gap is a robust finding; whether open-ended bias further increases with compression is less certain, sensitive to the judge that scores it. We address both with \textbf{QuantiBias}, a benchmark that pairs a generative, multilingual stereotype probe with the refusal and multiple-choice controls that isolate open-ended generation, contrasts each build with and without reasoning, and rates the content severity of what it generates. Across two backbone models (Qwen and Gemma), a five-family screen, and eight benchmarks, quantizers allocate their extra precision by capability data that carries no bias-prevention signal, and reasoning before answering roughly halves the effect on some families while doing nothing on others. A quantized build must be re-evaluated for open-ended bias, not only on the short-form safeguards it already passes.

</details>


### [47] [PrefReward: Learning User Preference Matrix for Personalized Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21067)
*Yue Wu,Chengbing Wang,Yimeng Bai,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PrefReward通过显式建模用户偏好矩阵并融入解码过程，提升LLM个性化生成的质量与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化方法依赖模型参数内的隐式表示，难以解释用户偏好且难以处理长上下文依赖。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1)提取用户专属偏好矩阵概括个体风格倾向；(2)基于KL散度奖励函数利用矩阵指导生成。

Result: 在LongLaMP数据集上，PrefReward在生成质量和个性化可解释性上优于非个性化及检索基线。

Conclusion: 显式偏好建模结合奖励引导解码能有效提升LLM个性化生成的性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable ability in generating personalized content by leveraging user histories and contextual cues. However, most existing personalization approaches rely on implicit representations within model parameters, making it difficult to interpret user-specific preferences or effectively handle long-context dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose PrefReward, a novel preference-aware generative framework that explicitly models user styles through a structured preference matrix and integrates it into the decoding process as a reward signal. PrefReward consists of two stages: (1) extracting a user-specific preference matrix that summarizes individual stylistic tendencies, and (2) using the matrix to guide generation via a KL-divergence-based reward function. Experiments on the LongLaMP dataset show that PrefReward outperforms non-personalized and retrieval-based baselines in both generation quality and personalization interpretability.

</details>


### [48] [One More Turn, Less Regret: A Regret-Based Multi-Turn Benchmark for LLMs' Clarification Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21143)
*Minh Ngoc Ta,My Anh Tran Nguyen,Duong D. Nguyen,Yuxia Wang,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RegretBench，一个多轮对话基准，用于评估大语言模型在面对模糊用户请求时的澄清策略行为，而非孤立的提问质量。通过隐含意图建模、语义状态跟踪和基于遗憾的目标函数，实验表明仅靠最终成功率不足以评估澄清效果，模型需在正确时机提出正确问题并适时停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法将澄清视为孤立的提问质量问题，忽略了澄清在多轮对话中的序贯决策本质：何时提问、问什么、何时停止、何时回答。缺乏能够联合衡量意图消解、交互成本、无效澄清和策略遗憾的基准。

Method: 构建 RegretBench 基准：1) 隐含意图形式化模糊性；2) 支持基于语义状态跟踪的自由交互；3) 引入基于遗憾的目标函数，度量模型相对于参考澄清策略的价值损失。在开放域问答和商品推荐两个场景进行实验。

Result: 实验显示，最终成功率相似的模型在效率、对用户行为的鲁棒性和停止决策上差异显著。RegretBench 能联合揭示模型是否有用且高效地进行澄清。

Conclusion: 有效的澄清不仅需要合理的问题，还要求模型在正确时机提出正确问题，并在用户意图明确后及时停止。RegretBench 为评估和改进对话助手的澄清策略提供了综合框架。

Abstract: Ambiguous user requests make clarification a sequential decision problem for conversational LLM assistants: they must decide whether to ask, what to ask, when to stop, and when to answer. We introduce RegretBench, a multi-turn benchmark that evaluates clarification as policy behavior rather than isolated question quality. RegretBench provides a hidden-intent formulation of ambiguity, supports free-form interaction grounded in semantic-state tracking, and introduces a regret-based objective that measures how much value a model loses relative to a reference clarification policy. Experiments on open-domain QA and product recommendation scenarios show that final success alone is insufficient, as models with similar accuracy can differ substantially in efficiency, robustness to user behaviors, and stopping decisions. By jointly measuring intent resolution, interaction cost, ineffective clarification, and regret, RegretBench reveals whether models clarify usefully and efficiently. Our results show that effective clarification requires more than plausible questions: models must ask the right question at the right time and stop once the user's intended meaning is clear.

</details>


### [49] [Progressive Cramming: Reliable Token Compression and What It Reveals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21231)
*Dmitrii Tarasov,Timofei Lashukov,Elizaveta Goncharova,Andrey Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出渐进式压缩方法，通过逐token增长目标前缀来探究压缩极限，发现完美重构是通过脆弱的引导而非可迁移语义实现的，不足以实现有意义的压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Token cramming方法采用固定token预算和99%准确率阈值，无法区分残余误差源于优化失败还是根本压缩极限，需要一种能动态探测压缩边界的方法。

Method: 引入渐进式压缩：目标前缀逐token增长，仅在固定优化预算内无法重构时停止；分析嵌入空间中的低维轨迹结构；通过因果注意力敲除干预，追踪预置压缩嵌入导致的性能下降在模型早期层的注意力交互机制。

Result: 渐进式轨迹在嵌入空间占据低维结构；预置压缩嵌入使多项选择基准准确率中等但持续下降，生成式评估中能力几乎完全崩溃；退化归因于压缩嵌入在模型早期层的注意力交互。

Conclusion: 渐进式压缩可作为研究压缩极限的工具；实验表明完美重构可通过脆弱的引导实现，而非可迁移的语义表示，因此完美重构不足以保证有意义的压缩。

Abstract: Token cramming compresses sequences into learned embeddings with near-perfect reconstruction, but fixed token budgets and 99\% accuracy thresholds leave it unclear whether residual errors reflect optimization failures or fundamental limits. We introduce progressive cramming, which grows the target prefix token-by-token, stopping only when reconstruction is no longer achievable within a fixed optimization budget. Progressive trajectories occupy low-dimensional structure in embedding space. Prepending a crammed embedding causes a moderate but consistent accuracy drop on multiple-choice benchmarks even with the original prefix in context, and collapses capability almost entirely under generative evaluation. Causal attention-knockout interventions trace this degradation to the embedding's interactions in the model's early layers. These results position progressive cramming as a tool for studying compression limits and show that perfect reconstruction - achievable through brittle steering rather than transferable semantics - is insufficient for meaningful compression.

</details>


### [50] [slang.gr as a Large-Scale Crowdsourced Resource for Non-Standard Greek](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21255)
*Panagiotis Papadakos,Katerina Papantoniou,Dimitris Plexousakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对希腊非标准语言众包词典 slang.gr 进行大规模计算研究，将嘈杂的民间标签映射为结构化多层分类体系，分析俚语语言结构与贡献者社区行为，发现俚语以人称和评价性语言为中心、形态创新性高、参与度高度倾斜且用户生命周期短，提出基于社区的置信度评分，证明基于分类的表示提升可解释性并保留行为结构，为非标准希腊语计算资源、社会语言学 NLP、偏见分析及 LLM 非正式语言研究奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俚语是日常语言核心组成部分，反映语言创造力、社会身份和文化变迁，但其动态非标准特性难以计算建模；现有资源缺乏大规模结构化分析，需建立希腊非标准语言的计算资源并探索社会语言学 NLP 等应用。

Method: 收集 slang.gr 词典的词汇内容、用户生成标签和交互数据；将噪声民间分类标签映射为捕捉语义类别和社会语言学元数据的结构化多层分类体系；基于该表示分析俚语语言结构（如人称/评价性语言中心性、形态创新性）和贡献者社区行为（参与度分布、用户生命周期、社区重叠）；设计融合用户角色、交互模式和审核信号的社区置信度评分机制。

Result: 发现希腊俚语强以人称和评价性语言为中心，展现高形态创造性，贡献者参与高度倾斜且用户生命周期短、社区重叠；基于分类的表示提升可解释性并保留行为结构，社区置信度评分有效整合多源信号。

Conclusion: 该工作为非标准希腊语建立了计算资源，验证了分类体系对俚语结构化分析的有效性，为社会语言学 NLP、偏见分析及大语言模型中非正式语言研究提供基础。

Abstract: Slang is a central component of everyday language, reflecting linguistic creativity, social identity, and cultural change, yet its dy- namic and non-standard nature makes it difficult to model computationally. We present the first large-scale computational study of slang.gr, a crowdsourced lexicon of Greek non-standard language, combining lexical content, user-generated tags, and interaction data. To enable the systematic analysis, we map noisy folksonomic tags to a structured multi-layer taxonomy capturing both semantic categories and sociolinguistic metadata. Using this representation, we analyze the linguistic structure of Greek slang and the behavior of its contributor community. We find that slang is strongly centered on person-related and evaluative language, exhibits high morphological creativity, and is shaped by highly skewed participation with short user lifespans and overlapping communities. Building on these signals, we introduce a community-based confidence score for definitions that integrates user roles, interaction patterns, and moderation signals. Our results show that taxonomy-based representations improve interpretability while retaining meaningful aspects of behavioral structure, enabling a more structured and interpretable analysis of confidence signals. Overall, this work establishes slang.gr as a computational resource for non-standard Greek and provides a foundation for sociolinguistic NLP, bias analysis, and the study of informal language in LLMs.

</details>


### [51] [A Comparative Evaluation of Embeddings and LLMs in a Greek Book Publisher Setting - The CUP Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21274)
*Katerina Papantoniou,Panagiotis Papadakos,Theodore Patkos,Dimitris Garefalakis,Nikos Vardakis,Dimitris Plexousakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CUP基准，包含868条希腊图书目录记录和104个专家标注查询，评估稀疏、稠密、混合及LLM辅助检索方法，发现多语言嵌入优于希腊语专用模型，混合检索整体最优；BM25擅长实体查询，稠密/混合方法在自然语言、噪声、跨语言和概念查询上表现更好；字段感知提示效果因模型而异，LLM目录摘要提升仅目录检索，LLM后过滤改善早期检索但成本高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏针对希腊语图书检索的真实世界评测基准，现有方法在词汇、语义、噪声及跨语言查询上的表现未被系统研究。

Method: 构建CUP基准（868条目录记录、104个专家标注分级相关性查询），对比BM25、句向量模型、混合检索及LLM辅助方法（字段感知提示、目录摘要、后过滤），进行查询级别细粒度分析。

Result: 多语言嵌入优于希腊语专用模型；混合检索整体最优；BM25在实体查询上最强；稠密/混合方法在自然语言、噪声、跨语言、概念查询上提升显著；字段感知提示效果因模型异；LLM目录摘要改善仅目录检索；LLM后过滤提升早期检索但计算成本高。

Conclusion: CUP为希腊语检索提供了跨词汇、语义、噪声、跨语言查询的真实评测平台，揭示了不同检索范式在各类查询上的优劣势，指导未来多语言图书检索系统设计。

Abstract: We present CUP, a Greek book retrieval benchmark consisting of 868 catalog records and 104 expert-annotated queries with graded relevance judgments. We evaluate sparse (BM25), dense (sentence-transformers), hybrid, and LLM-assisted retrieval methods in this book-search setting. Multilingual embeddings outperform Greek-specific models, while hybrid retrieval performs best overall. A query-level analysis shows that BM25 excels at named-entity queries, while dense and hybrid methods improve natural-language, noisy, cross-lingual, and concept queries. Field-aware prompting has model-specific effects, while LLM TOC summarization improves TOC-only retrieval and LLM post-filtering improves early-stage retrieval at a high cost. Overall, CUP enables real-world evaluation of Greek retrieval across lexical, semantic, noisy, and cross-lingual queries.

</details>


### [52] [A Unified Moral-Value Dataset for Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21279)
*Zhaohui Zeng,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个统一的道德价值数据集，用于指令微调以实现大语言模型与人类价值观的对齐，并研究了混合数据集比例对通用任务和价值导向任务性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管指令微调在零样本任务中表现出潜力，可作为价值对齐的有效途径，但现有指令微调数据集并非专门围绕道德场景和行为设计，缺乏针对道德价值的专用数据资源。

Method: 将现有的道德价值数据集合并为统一语料库，并转换为指令-响应格式，构建统一道德价值数据集；将其与通用任务数据集按不同比例混合，进行指令微调实验，评估通用任务性能保持情况及价值导向任务性能变化。

Result: 实验表明，混合数据集训练能保持通用任务性能，且混合比例会影响价值导向任务的性能，提供了初步观察结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可直接用于指令微调的道德价值数据集，为后续价值对齐研究提供了有用资源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have developed rapidly and become valuable tools in everyday life. However, how to align LLMs to a particular set of human values is still an open problem. Recent studies show that instruction tuning has strong potential for zero-shot tasks and may serve as an effective approach to addressing value alignment. Nevertheless, although many datasets for instruction tuning already exist, they are not specifically designed around moral scenarios and behaviors. We construct a unified moral-value dataset that can be directly used for instruction tuning. This dataset is built upon existing moral-value datasets by merging them into a unified corpus and converting them into an instruction-response format. We show that training on a mixed dataset combining general task datasets with our dataset preserves general-task performance, and we report preliminary observations on how the mixing ratio affects value-oriented task performance. Our work provides a moral-value dataset for instruction tuning and offers a useful resource for further alignment research. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/teohzzh/value-for-instruction-tuning.

</details>


### [53] [news-crawler-LM: A Small Long-Context Model For High-Quality News Crawling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21284)
*Pascal Stolzenburg,Jonas Golde,Max Dallabetta,Alan Akbik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种小型长上下文语言模型 news-crawler-LM，在 Fundus 库上微调，将原始 HTML 转为纯文本和结构化 JSON，在 HTML-to-Markdown 和 HTML-to-JSON 任务上显著优于基线，但在 HTML-to-plaintext 任务上仅略优于规则库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻页面结构提取面临 HTML 布局异构、标记不一致、大量样板代码等挑战；规则爬虫需人工配置难以泛化，大模型计算成本高，因此需要一种轻量、灵活且高效的模型。

Method: 基于 Fundus 新闻爬取库的人工验证高质量提取数据，微调一个小型长上下文语言模型 news-crawler-LM，将原始 HTML 直接转换为纯文本和结构化 JSON（含标题、作者、发布日期、正文等字段）。

Result: 在 HTML-to-Markdown 任务上 BLEU 提升 4.8，METEOR 提升 6.1；在 HTML-to-JSON 任务上 BLEU 提升 2.2，METEOR 提升 4.1；但在 HTML-to-plaintext 任务上对未见发布商仅略优于规则解析库。

Conclusion: news-crawler-LM 在结构化提取任务上表现优异，计算成本低，适合实际部署；但纯文本提取任务改进有限，未来可探索进一步提升泛化能力。已开源模型和工件。

Abstract: Extracting structured content from news pages remains challenging due to heterogeneous HTML layouts, inconsistent markup, and substantial boilerplate such as navigation elements and advertisements. Rule-based news crawlers can achieve high extraction accuracy by encoding site-specific structure, but require manual configuration in order to generalize to new publishers. Large language models provide a more flexible alternative by reducing the need for handcrafted rules, but their high computational cost limits practical deployment. In this paper, we introduce news-crawler-LM, a small long-context language model fine-tuned on high-quality, human-validated extractions from the Fundus news-crawling library. Our model converts raw HTML into plaintext and structured JSON, including fields such as headline, author, publication date, and article body. In our experiments, news-crawler-LM outperforms strong baselines in HTML-to-Markdown and HTML-to-JSON extraction, improving performance by +4.8 BLEU and +6.1 METEOR in the HTML-to-Markdown task, and by +2.2 BLEU and +4.1 METEOR in the HTML-to-JSON task. However, we also observe that our model only slightly better compared to other rule-based parsing libraries on the HTML-to-plaintext task in evaluations on previously unseen publishers. We release all models and artifacts to the research community.

</details>


### [54] [Adaptive Depth Sparse Framework: Similarity-Driven Resource Allocation for Pre-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21291)
*Yidu Wu,Xiang Wang,Kejie Zhao,Zhangchi Wang,Qinghai Guo,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出AdaDSF框架，通过自适应深度稀疏化将预训练LLM转化为稀疏模型，无需完全重训练，利用层间余弦相似度分配token保留率，轻量级路由选择信息token，特征保持对齐目标匹配稀疏与稠密表示，在GPT-NeoX和Qwen2.5上显著降低推理FLOPs且性能接近稠密模型，优于MoD、D-LLM、DLO等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构推理成本高，现有加速方法依赖任务特定微调或从头训练，增加适应成本并限制跨任务通用性。需要一种无需完全重训练即可将现成预训练LLM转化为深度稀疏模型的方法。

Method: AdaDSF框架：1) 基于层输入输出隐藏状态的余弦相似度表征层贡献差异，据此分配逐层token保留比率；2) 轻量级路由器在每层选择信息量大的token；3) 引入特征保持对齐目标，匹配稀疏与稠密模型的中间和最终表示。

Result: 在GPT-NeoX和Qwen2.5上进行语言建模和常识推理实验，AdaDSF大幅降低推理FLOPs，性能保持接近稠密模型。在可比稀疏度下，AdaDSF准确率下降小于MoD、D-LLM、DLO等强基线。

Conclusion: AdaDSF通过自适应深度稀疏化实现了高效LLM推理，无需完全重训练即可将预训练模型转化为稀疏模型，显著降低计算成本同时保持性能，优于现有基线方法，具有跨任务通用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong generation and reasoning performance, but the Transformer architecture incurs high inference cost. Existing acceleration methods often rely on task-specific fine-tuning or training from scratch, increasing adaptation cost and limiting cross-task usability. We present an Adaptive Depth Sparse Framework (AdaDSF) that converts off-the-shelf pre-trained LLMs into depth-sparse models without full retraining. Our key insight is that layers contribute unequally to representation transformation, characterized by the cosine similarity between layer input and output hidden states. Based on this, AdaDSF assigns layer-wise token retention ratios from similarity statistics, uses a lightweight router to select informative tokens at each layer, and introduces a feature-preserving alignment objective to match intermediate and final representations between sparse and dense models. On GPT-NeoX and Qwen2.5 over language modeling and commonsense reasoning, AdaDSF substantially reduces inference FLOPs while preserving performance close to dense counterparts. Under comparable sparsity, AdaDSF consistently yields smaller accuracy degradation than strong baselines including MoD, D-LLM, and DLO.

</details>


### [55] [GRADRAG: Cross-Component Prompt Adaptation for Coordinated Multi-Agent RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21324)
*Paolo Pedinotti,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GRADRAG 是一个跨组件提示词适应框架，将 RAG 流水线建模为计算图，通过评估器反馈迭代优化上游代理（检索器、图构建器、回答器），实现端到端协同优化，在 SQUALITY 和 QMSUM 基准上比仅优化最终生成器的基线提升 12-15 个百分点偏好优势，且大多在两轮迭代内收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 系统多采用多 LLM 代理，但以往工作多孤立优化单个组件，缺乏跨流水线协同改进机制，导致整体性能受限。

Method: 将 RAG 流水线建模为计算图，引入评估器对下游答案及支撑证据进行结构化评判，生成可执行反馈；提示词优化器据此迭代更新上游自适应代理（检索器、图构建器、回答器）；评估器同时支持早停机制。实验覆盖平铺块检索（IRCoT 查询细化）与图检索（实体关系图迭代构建）两种范式。

Result: 在 SQUALITY 与 QMSUM 两个基准上，GRADRAG 在两种检索范式下均显著优于仅优化最终生成器的单步细化基线，LLM 判断的成对比较净偏好优势达 12-15 个百分点，且主要增益在两轮迭代内实现。

Conclusion: GRADRAG 验证了跨组件提示词适应在多代理 RAG 系统中的有效性，通过结构化反馈闭环与早停机制，以少量迭代即可大幅提升端到端生成质量，为 RAG 系统协同优化提供了通用范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems increasingly employ multiple LLM agents. Yet, most prior work optimizes components in isolation rather than coordinating improvements across the pipeline. We introduce GRADRAG, a framework for cross-component prompt adaptation that models the RAG pipeline as a computational graph and propagates structured evaluation feedback to update upstream agents. An Evaluator critiques downstream answers and supporting evidence, producing actionable feedback that a Prompt Optimizer uses to iteratively update adaptive agents, such as retrievers, graph constructors, and answerers. The Evaluator also triggers early stopping when the output is deemed satisfactory. We evaluate GRADRAG on the SQUALITY and QMSUM benchmarks under two retrieval paradigms: flat chunk-based retrieval using IRCoT-style query refinement (Trivedi et al., 2023), and graph-based retrieval that constructs and iteratively enriches an entity-relation graph from the document. Across both settings, GRADRAG consistently outperforms one-step refinement baselines that update only the final generator, achieving a 12-15 percentage point net preference margin in LLM-judged pairwise comparisons, with most gains realized within two refinement iterations.

</details>


### [56] [Phonetic forced alignment for low-resource language varieties: Model training and evaluation on Chengdu Mandarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21332)
*Zhiheng Qian,Aini Li,Hai Hu,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对低资源语言变体（成都话）的语音强制对齐，利用17小时语料库和自定义G2P词典，训练了文本相关的GMM-HMM模型（Chengdu-MFA）和微调预训练音频编码器的文本无关模型（Chengdu-FC），两者均显著优于标准普通话基线，分别将平均音素边界差异降低31.8%和61.2%，建立了一套无需人工标注的自举流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音强制对齐系统缺乏针对低资源语言变体的专用模型，导致对成都话等方言的对齐准确性不足。

Method: 基于17小时成都话语料库和自定义G2P词典，训练文本相关的GMM-HMM模型（Chengdu-MFA）；利用Chengdu-MFA生成的伪标签微调预训练音频编码器，实现文本无关的帧分类对齐（Chengdu-FC）。

Result: 在专家标注测试集上，Chengdu-MFA将平均音素边界差异降低31.8%，Chengdu-FC降低61.2%，均显著优于标准普通话基线。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种实用的自举流程，能够在无需耗时费力的人工标注情况下，为低资源语言变体开发高精度的强制对齐器。

Abstract: Phonetic forced alignment is a key technique in phonetic research, yet existing alignment systems lack specialized models for low-resource language varieties. We address this by training text-dependent and text-independent aligners for Chengdu Mandarin using a 17-hour corpus and a custom G2P dictionary. We trained a text-dependent GMM-HMM model (Chengdu-MFA) and fine-tuned a pretrained audio encoder on frame classification with Chengdu-MFA's pseudo label for text-independent alignment (Chengdu-FC). Evaluation on an expert-annotated test set show that both methods significantly outperform Standard Mandarin baselines. Chengdu-MFA reduced average phone boundary differences by 31.8%, while Chengdu-FC achieved a 61.2% reduction. This work establishes a practical bootstrapping pipeline for developing accurate aligners for under-resourced varieties without labor- and time-intensive manual annotation.

</details>


### [57] [Capital Markets LLM Reliability Score (CM-LRS): From Plausible to Bankable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21340)
*Prerit Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CM-LRS（资本市场 LLM 可靠性评分），在工作流输出层面从七个维度评估大模型在资本市场场景下的可银行性；实验显示闭源前沿模型得分接近，开源模型落后，差距主要集中在检索与综合环节，决策有用性维度区分度最高且评委一致性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多关注问答层面的表层准确或数值推理，缺乏对从业者需在监管环境下实际捍卫的工作流级输出（如条款提取、先例检索、发行人画像、可比交易推理等）的系统性评估。

Method: 构建七维评分量表（事实准确性、证据可追溯性、数值一致性、工作流完整性、来源规范性、决策有用性、可复核/可审计性），每维 0-5 分，锚定监管审查信号；在五个真实/合成资本市场工作流上，用四个跨家族的 LLM 评委对四个模型打分并取平均。

Result: 1) 四大闭源模型平均 CM-LRS 差异仅 0.22 分（Sonnet 4.6=4.31, Opus 4.7=4.30, GPT-5.5=4.09），开源基线 Llama 3.3 70B=3.15 垫底。2) 差距主要来自检索（2.23 分）与综合（2.15 分），而非抽取（0.84 分）。3) 决策有用性维度跨模型方差最大（发行人画像达 4.0 分），且评委间一致性最高（均值 r=0.52）。

Conclusion: 流畅性易得，可银行性才是门槛。CM-LRS 为监管级工作流提供了可调、可审、多维的量化标尺，揭示了当前模型在检索与综合环节的短板，指导后续针对性对齐与工程落地。

Abstract: In capital-markets workflows the question is rarely whether a large language model can produce a fluent draft, but whether the draft is bankable: defensible in front of a counter-party or a regulator, with the documents in hand. Existing methods address parts of that gap: open-domain QA benchmarks reward surface accuracy, and finance benchmarks (FinanceBench, FinQA, ConvFinQA) advance document-grounded and numerical QA but evaluate at the question-answer layer rather than the workflow outputs practitioners defend.
  We introduce CM-LRS, a Capital Markets LLM Reliability Score, evaluating outputs at the workflow-output layer across seven dimensions: factual accuracy, evidence traceability, numerical consistency, workflow completeness, source discipline, decision usefulness, and reviewability/auditability. Each is scored 0-5 against a rubric anchored on signals reviewers in regulated settings use; the aggregate is tunable to the workflow.
  We demonstrate CM-LRS on five workflows (DCM transaction-terms extraction, precedent retrieval, issuer profile synthesis, M&A transaction-comparable reasoning, ECM transaction-terms extraction) over public SEC EDGAR filings, a public UK takeover release, and fictional synthetic supplements, scoring four models against four independent LLM judges spanning three model families.
  Three findings. First, the frontier closed-source models cluster within 0.22 points on four-judge averaged CM-LRS (Sonnet 4.6 = 4.31, Opus 4.7 = 4.30, GPT-5.5 = 4.09); all four judges place the open-weights baseline (Llama 3.3 70B = 3.15) last. Second, that gap concentrates on retrieval (2.23) and synthesis (2.15), not extraction (0.84). Third, Decision Usefulness shows the widest cross-model dispersion of any dimension (4.0 points on issuer profiling) and top-tier inter-judge agreement (mean r = 0.52).
  Plausibility is cheap. Bankability is the bar.

</details>


### [58] [Word meaning co-determines vowel-inherent spectral change. A corpus-based investigation of conversational Mandarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21391)
*Xiaoyun Jin,Mirjam Ernestus,R. Harald Baayen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现普通话自发会话中元音共振峰轨迹包含词特定的语义信息，F1和F2轨迹可由上下文化词嵌入预测，挑战了模块化语音产出模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验词汇语义是否共同决定发音的精细细节，从而挑战认知语音产出的模块化模型。

Method: 使用广义可加模型（GAM）和分布语义的词嵌入，控制元音时长、性别、说话人身份、共同发音、元音身份和话语位置等变量，分析自发会话普通话中的元音固有谱变（VISC）。

Result: 在控制混杂变量后，词语的F1和F2轨迹仍可从其上下文化嵌入中以显著高于排列基线的准确度预测，表明元音共振峰动态包含与语境意义相关的词特定成分。

Conclusion: 词汇语义共同决定了发音的精细声学细节，语音产出并非严格模块化，而是语义与发音动态相互交织。

Abstract: This study investigates vowel-inherent spectral change (VISC) in spontaneous conversational Mandarin. Using the generalized additive model and word embeddings from distributional semantics, we show that, when controlling for variables such as vowel duration, gender, speaker identity, co-articulation, vowel identity, and utterance position, vowel formant trajectory dynamics have word-specific components that are tied to their meaning in context: The F1 and F2 trajectories of words can be predicted from their contextualized embeddings with an accuracy that substantially exceeds a permutation baseline. Challenging modular cognitive models of speech production, these results indicate that, words' semantics co-determine the fine details of their articulation.

</details>


### [59] [MemTools: A Unified Research Framework for Interoperable Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21404)
*Chengfeng Zhao,Jinhui Chen,Sirui Liang,Shizhu He,Yequan Wang,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemTools 是一个互操作性研究框架，解耦记忆系统组件与部署环境，通过声明式数据契约标准化记忆生命周期，将基准数据集与执行协议正交分离，并提供统一计算接口协调符号、神经和多模态记忆表示，实现跨系统组件集成、评估协议重配置和异构记忆协调的系统性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统存在架构碎片化问题：记忆生命周期各阶段耦合、评估逻辑与特定数据集纠缠、对异构记忆类型管理支持有限，限制了系统性研究。

Method: 提出 MemTools 框架：1) 使用声明式数据契约标准化记忆生命周期，实现组件跨系统可互换组装；2) 将基准数据集与执行协议正交分离，支持受控评估；3) 提供统一计算接口，在共享运行时协调符号、神经和多模态记忆表示。

Result: 实证评估表明，MemTools 在跨系统组件集成、评估协议重配置和异构记忆协调三方面均能实现记忆设计变量的系统性隔离与分析。

Conclusion: MemTools 为推进智能体记忆的原则性研究提供了实用且可扩展的基础设施。

Abstract: While memory systems are essential for agent architectures, pervasive architectural fragmentation restricts systematic research. Existing implementations typically couple different stages of the memory lifecycle, entangle evaluation logic with specific datasets, and provide limited support for the management of heterogeneous memory types. We introduce MemTools, an interoperability research framework that decouples memory system components from their underlying deployment environments. MemTools standardizes the memory lifecycle through declarative data contracts, enabling the interchangeable assembly of components across different systems. It orthogonally separates benchmark datasets from execution protocols to facilitate controlled assessments. Furthermore, MemTools provides a unified computational interface for coordinating symbolic, neural, and multimodal memory representations within a shared runtime. Empirical evaluations on cross-system component integration, evaluation protocol reconfiguration, and heterogeneous memory coordination demonstrate that MemTools enables systematic isolation and analysis of memory design variables. These findings suggest that MemTools provides a practical and extensible infrastructure for advancing principled research on agent memory.

</details>


### [60] [Anti-Periodic Positional Encoding: Möbius Boundary Conditions Make In-Context Retrieval Reliable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21405)
*Ji Ho Bae*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Möbius RoPE 基于反周期频率梯度，实现位置全纯度 -1，将序列两端确定性耦合，通过在 25% 头上混合 Möbius 频率，在不增加困惑度的情况下大幅提升长上下文针 haystack 检索的可靠性，且仅需一行频率表替换即可获得零成本的检索稳定性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的旋转位置编码（RoPE）在长上下文检索任务中表现不稳定，存在“检索种子彩票”问题，即不同随机种子下检索性能方差极大；作者旨在设计一种反周期边界条件，使序列两端确定性耦合，从而在保持基线困惑度的同时提升检索可靠性。

Method: 提出 Möbius RoPE，采用反周期频率阶梯 θ_i = π(2i+1)/N，使每个旋转平面在训练上下文中推进 π 的奇数倍，位置全纯度为 -1，通过闭式 Dirichlet “偶极子” 将序列两端确定性耦合。实验中采用混合策略：在 25% 的注意力头上使用 Möbius 频率，其余头保持标准 RoPE。在 160M 和 410M 规模模型上各预训练 2B FineWeb-Edu tokens，对比标准 RoPE、非周期阶梯、周期阶梯（全纯度 +1）及 NoPE 等基线。

Result: 混合模型困惑度基本持平（29.66 vs 29.72），但针 haystack 检索可靠性大幅提升：在上下文 512 下，6 个种子平均准确率 90.3±5.7% 对比 63.3±31.4%，最差种子 86% 对比 14%，方差检验 p=0.013-0.029；410M 规模复现（Levene p=0.040）。消融实验显示：同频带非周期阶梯无效，周期阶梯仅部分有效；冻结权重将频率表换回标准 RoPE 会导致检索崩溃，损害集中在远距离针。NoPE 短上下文更可靠但困惑度增加 13% 且外推最差。仅反周期混合方案同时实现基线困惑度与高可靠性下限。效果局限于训练窗口内的单针检索；一行频率替换即提供零成本的检索稳定性保险。

Conclusion: Möbius RoPE 引入了首个位置编码中的反周期边界条件，通过确定性耦合序列两端，在不牺牲语言建模困惑度的前提下，显著提升了长上下文单针检索的稳定性和最差种子表现，且实现极其简单（仅需修改频率表），为解决“检索种子彩票”问题提供了零成本的工程化方案。

Abstract: Möbius RoPE is a rotary positional encoding built on the anti-periodic frequency ladder $θ_i=π(2i+1)/N$: every rotation plane advances by an odd multiple of $π$ across the training context, so the positional holonomy is $-1$ and the two ends of the sequence are deterministically coupled through a closed-form Dirichlet "dipole"; to our knowledge this is the first anti-periodic boundary condition in positional encoding. We verify the theory numerically to $\sim 10^{-6}$ and pretrain 48 models spanning six 160M-class and three 410M-class arms (2B FineWeb-Edu tokens each; the hybrid arm puts Möbius frequencies on 25% of heads). Hybrid perplexity is unchanged (29.66 vs. 29.72), but needle-in-a-haystack retrieval becomes reliable: $90.3\pm5.7\%$ versus $63.3\pm31.4\%$ at context 512 ($n=6$ seeds), observed worst seed 86% versus 14%, robust variance tests $p=0.013$-$0.029$ (unadjusted), recurring at 410M (Levene $p=0.040$). Matched controls isolate the mechanism: an aperiodic ladder in the same frequency band reproduces none of the effect, and a periodic (holonomy $+1$) ladder only a fraction. Swapping trained models' frequency table back to standard RoPE (weights frozen) collapses retrieval, with damage concentrated on far needles: trained models depend on this long-range geometry. A NoPE arm is even more reliable at short context but pays a 13% perplexity tax and extrapolates worst; only the anti-periodic hybrid pairs baseline perplexity with a high reliability floor. The effect is scoped to single-needle retrieval within the training window; a one-line frequency swap thus provides zero-cost insurance against the retrieval seed lottery.

</details>


### [61] [An Evaluation Framework for Structured Audio Captions Validated by Controlled Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21424)
*Liang-Yuan Wu,Sripathi Sridhar,Mark Cartwright,Magdalena Fuentes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多轴评估框架，用于结构化音频描述的自动评估，结合大语言模型判断语义细微差别和确定性计算指标测量声学偏差，并通过可控扰动测试验证了框架的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字幕评估指标侧重于平坦的文本输出，无法可靠评估结构化音频描述中的多模态属性，因此需要一种针对结构化音频描述的多轴评估框架。

Method: 基于AudioCards数据集，在五个正交轴（标签集、描述、逻辑推理、数值测量、频谱概况）上评估输出。结合大语言模型裁判捕捉语义细微差别，以及确定性计算指标精确测量声学偏差。引入可控扰动测试协议，向真实标注注入分类分级错误以验证框架可靠性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架能成功区分保留语义的意译与真正的语义和声学损坏。

Conclusion: 提出的多轴评估框架有效解决了结构化音频描述的评估难题，结合LLM语义判断与计算声学指标，通过扰动测试证明了其鲁棒性和判别能力。

Abstract: Recent advancements in automated audio captioning (AAC) have shifted from monolithic sentence generation toward structured formats that explicitly disentangle distinct acoustic and semantic properties. However, evaluating this heterogeneous data remains a significant challenge. Existing caption metrics focus on flat textual outputs and fail to reliably assess multimodal attributes. To bridge this gap, we propose a multi-axis evaluation framework tailored for structured audio descriptions. Building on the AudioCards dataset, we evaluate outputs across five orthogonal axes: tag-sets, descriptions, logical reasoning, numeric measurements, and spectral profiles. Our approach combines Large Language Model (LLM) judges to capture semantic nuance with deterministic computational metrics to precisely measure acoustic deviations. To rigorously validate the reliability of this framework, we introduce a controlled perturbation testing protocol that injects typed, graded errors into groundtruth annotations. Our results demonstrate that this framework successfully distinguishes meaning-preserving paraphrases from genuine semantic and acoustic corruptions.

</details>


### [62] [Token Budget Saturation and Mechanistic Early Detection of Reasoning Non-Convergence in Chain-of-Thought Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21433)
*Renuka Oladri,Niveda Jawahar,Abdirisak Mohamed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B等思维链推理模型的双峰收敛模式，发现收敛生成准确率高达90.3%，非收敛仅6.6%。通过在隐藏状态激活上训练线性探针，发现第20层第150个token的激活可预测收敛命运（AUC 0.608），且早于生成结束。探针优于行为基线，排列检验p=0.063。表明收敛命运在中间表示中提前编码，为早退出推理和自适应计算分配提供路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链推理模型表现出双峰收敛模式：要么在token预算内终止（收敛），要么耗尽预算无结论（非收敛）。作者旨在表征这一现象，并探究是否能在思维链早期通过内部模型表示检测收敛结果，从而实现早退出推理和自适应计算分配。

Method: 实证分析收敛与非收敛生成在AIME 1983-2024上的准确率差异；在token位置50-300的隐藏状态激活上训练线性探针，评估预测收敛命运的能力；使用5折交叉验证计算AUC；与基于token熵和重复统计的行为基线比较；进行扫描层面的排列检验（100,000次排列）评估统计显著性。

Result: 收敛生成准确率90.3%，非收敛仅6.6%，整体收敛率62.0%。第20层第150个token激活达到AUC 0.608（±0.080），甚至在token 50就显著高于随机。激活探针持续优于行为基线。排列检验p=0.063，提示存在微弱信号但样本量不足以在常规阈值下确认。

Conclusion: 收敛命运在生成结束前就部分编码在中间表示中，这为早退出推理和自适应计算分配开辟了道路。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B exhibit a bimodal convergence pattern: generations either terminate within a token budget (converged) or exhaust it without reaching a conclusion (non-converged). We characterize this phenomenon empirically, showing that converged generations achieve 90.3% accuracy on AIME 1983-2024 while non-converged ones achieve only 6.6%, with an overall convergence rate of 62.0%. We then ask whether this outcome is detectable early in the thinking chain using internal model representations. Training linear probes on hidden-state activations at token positions 50-300, we find that layer-20 activations at token 150 achieve AUC 0.608 (+-0.080, 5-fold CV), reliably above chance even at token 50. Activation probes consistently outperform behavioral baselines derived from token entropy and repetition statistics. A sweep-level permutation test yields p=0.063 (100,000 permutations), consistent with a modest signal that our sample size cannot confirm at conventional thresholds. These findings suggest that convergence fate is partially encoded in intermediate representations well before the generation ends, opening a path toward early-exit inference and adaptive compute allocation.

</details>


### [63] [When Trivia Is Not Trivial: Everyday Knowledge Failures in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21445)
*Anna Mosolova,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TriviaRoomQA多语言基准测试，评估LLM在日常文化、长尾知识上的表现，发现模型在学术性知识（历史、地理、数学）强，但在流行文化（名人、音乐、电影、新闻）弱，且跨语言表现不一，揭示了现有学术饱和基准未捕捉的知识鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多聚焦学术性、事实性知识，缺乏对日常文化、流行文化及长尾知识的系统评估，且多语言下的知识获取一致性未被充分探究。

Method: 构建TriviaRoomQA基准，包含288个主题，3300道六语言并行多选题及5340道法语专用题；评估30个开源LLM（7-70B参数），覆盖欧亚美供应商，对比不同主题、语言下的表现。

Result: 模型在历史、地理、数学等知识密集型主题表现优异；在名人、音乐、电影、新闻等日常流行文化主题显著较弱；同一问题在不同语言下表现差异大，事实知识获取非语言无关。

Conclusion: TriviaRoomQA揭示了LLM在文化接地、长尾知识上的系统性短板，且跨语言知识访问存在不一致，现有学术饱和基准无法捕捉这一鸿沟，呼吁构建更贴近真实世界知识分布的评测体系。

Abstract: Quiz rooms, trivia nights, and quiz shows challenge human knowledge across a wide range of topics, from canonical facts to everyday culture. In this paper, we examine whether large language models (LLMs) can perform competitively in such settings, using quiz-style questions to test them on both common and niche topics. We introduce TriviaRoomQA, a multilingual benchmark designed to evaluate everyday, culturally grounded, and long-tail knowledge across 288 topics. The benchmark contains 3,300 parallel multiple-choice questions in six European languages and additional 5,340 French-only questions for a more fine-grained case study. We evaluate 30 open-weight LLMs from European, Asian, and North American providers, covering models from 7 to 70B parameters. We find that models are strong on knowledge-intensive topics such as history, geography, and mathematics, but substantially weaker on everyday popular-culture topics such as celebrities, music, movies, and news. Moreover, model performance varies across languages even for the same underlying questions, suggesting that access to factual knowledge is not always language-independent. In sum, our dataset and experiments demonstrate an important knowledge gap which is not captured by existing academic-based saturated benchmarks.

</details>


### [64] [RUMBA: Russian User Memory Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21447)
*Elizaveta Shevtsova,Inna Glebkina,Mark Baushenko,Pavel Gulyaev,Alena Fenogenova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 RUMBA，首个面向俄语的长期对话记忆基准，包含细粒度记忆题型分类与统一评估方法，旨在解决现有基准英语为主、仅用聚合检索指标、忽视长跨度上下文/时序信息/推理交互的问题；实验表明 RUMBA 能诊断不同记忆机制的优劣与失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆基准以英语为中心，依赖聚合检索指标，无法捕捉长跨度上下文、时间信息与推理之间的交互，缺乏细粒度、多维度的评估体系。

Method: 构建带时间戳的用户-助手多轮对话及跨会话 QA 对，设计细粒度记忆题型分类法，统一方法学同时考量语义类型、会话范围、时序推理、时间表达显性度四个维度；提供俄语主集与对齐英语子集。

Result: 在主流记忆系统与长上下文模型上评测，RUMBA 能按基准切片诊断模型行为，揭示不同记忆机制的优势与失效模式。

Conclusion: RUMBA 填补了非英语长期对话记忆评估空白，提供了可解释、可诊断的细粒度基准，推动长期记忆机制的系统性分析与改进。

Abstract: The ability to handle long-term memory in LLMs is becoming increasingly critical, yet existing benchmarks remain English-centric and rely on aggregate retrieval metrics, failing to capture interactions between long-range context, temporal information, and reasoning. To address this, we introduce RUMBA (Russian User Memory BenchmArk) - a new benchmark for long-term conversational memory that provides a fine-grained taxonomy of memory-centric question types and a unified methodology accounting for semantic type, session scope, temporal reasoning, and the explicitness of temporal expressions. RUMBA consists of timestamped user-assistant dialogues with QA pairs requiring retrieval, combination, and reasoning across sessions. While designed for Russian, we also provide an aligned English subset under the same methodology. We evaluate contemporary memory systems and long-context models, and show how RUMBA serves as a diagnostic tool to analyze model behavior across benchmark slices and identify strengths and failure modes of different memory mechanisms.

</details>


### [65] [What, Where, and How: Disentangling the Roles of Task, Language, and Model in Code Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21491)
*Piotr Wilam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究独立训练的语言模型是否以相同方式表示相同概念，采用2×2设计（Python/Rust × Qwen2.5-Coder-7B/DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B）并提取概念电路。发现：概念获得专用电路的“内容”由任务决定（模型间高度一致），但电路的“位置”和“增长方式”由模型决定（Qwen在后期层，DeepSeek在早期层）。电路普遍性仅存在于表示内容，不存在于计算组织。Rust构造获得的电路是Python的2-3倍；两模型均跨语言共享神经元，DeepSeek共享更多；Qwen将Rust类型/特征关键词绑定为紧密神经元簇。消融实验和线性探针验证电路功能性。结论仅限此2×2设计，下一步测试模型画像能否预测第三模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究独立训练的大语言模型在代码理解任务中，是否会以相同方式表示相同语法概念，即概念表示的普遍性问题。此前缺乏系统性的跨模型、跨语言对比来分离任务、语言、模型三个因素的影响。

Method: 采用概念电路提取方法，构建2×2实验设计：两种编程语言（Python、Rust）与两种模型（Qwen2.5-Coder-7B、DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B）。在四个单元中统一测量58个Python和57个Rust语法概念的电路。通过Spearman相关系数量化模型间电路分配一致性，分析电路跨层分布位置与增长模式，计算跨语言神经元共享比例及Jaccard相似度，并用消融实验和线性探针验证电路功能性。

Result: 1. 任务决定‘什么’概念获得电路：两模型在Python（ρ=0.638）和Rust（ρ=0.673）上高度一致（p<10⁻⁷）。2. 模型决定‘哪里’：Qwen电路集中在L17-19，DeepSeek在L6-7。3. 模型决定‘怎么’：Qwen原子概念有早期峰值，DeepSeek无。4. Rust构造获得电路量是Python的2-3倍。5. 两模型均跨语言共享神经元（6/7和7/7配对构造），DeepSeek共享度为Qwen的1.94倍。6. Qwen将9个Rust类型/特征关键词绑定为紧密簇（Jaccard 0.535 vs 零假设0.112, p<0.001）。消融与线性探针确认电路功能性。

Conclusion: 电路普遍性仅存在于表示内容（‘是什么’），不存在于计算组织（‘在哪里’、‘如何增长’）。模型间在概念电路分配上达成中等一致（ρ≈0.65），但层级位置与动态模式完全由模型架构/训练决定。Rust更丰富的类型系统引致更多专用电路。跨语言神经元共享揭示模型内部抽象的语言无关性。Qwen对Rust类型/特征关键词的聚类显示其捕捉到语法表层不可见的语义维度。所有结论限于此2×2设计，需进一步测试模型画像的泛化预测力。

Abstract: Do independently trained language models come to represent the same thing in the same way? We answer for code, extending a recently introduced concept-circuit extraction method to a 2x2 design -- Python and Rust crossed with Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B -- and measuring a complete inventory of grammatical concepts (58 Python, 57 Rust) identically in all four cells: the smallest design that separates what depends on the task, the language, and the model.
  The answer splits into three parts. What earns dedicated circuitry is set by the task: the models agree on which concepts receive circuits (Spearman $ρ$ = 0.638 for Python, 0.673 for Rust, both p < $10^{-7}$). Where those circuits sit is set by the model: Qwen processes concepts in a late band (~L17-19), DeepSeek at L6-7, for both languages. How circuits grow across layers is also set by the model: Qwen gives its atomic concepts an early spike that DeepSeek does not. "Are circuits universal?" thus has no single answer: yes for What, no for Where and How -- universality is a property of representational content, not of computational organisation.
  None of this structure was fixed in advance. The agreement could have landed anywhere between independence and identity; it lands at $ρ\approx 0.65$. Rust constructs receive 2-3x more concept-specific circuitry than their Python equivalents, in both models. Both models share neurons between the languages (6/7 and 7/7 paired constructs), DeepSeek 1.94x more than Qwen -- a direction no prior result predicts. And Qwen binds nine keywords of Rust's type-and-trait machinery into one tight neuron cluster (Jaccard 0.535 vs null 0.112, p < 0.001), a semantic dimension invisible in surface syntax. Ablation and linear probes confirm the circuits are functional.
  All claims are scoped to this 2x2; whether the per-model profile predicts a third model is the designed next test.

</details>


### [66] [Artificial Epanorthosis: Why large language models overuse a classical rhetorical figure, and how to mitigate it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21498)
*Federico Boggia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型中反复修正修辞（epanorthosis）的系统性过度使用现象，提出 Epanorthosis Index 量化指标，发现模型在演讲体裁中过度使用（约 2-3 倍），在非正式问答中使用不足，而在论辩、新闻、百科体裁中与人类相当。通过轻量级 LoRA 适配器和指令微调可有效校准至人类基线，主张目标是按体裁校准而非消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cicero 和 Quintilian 两千年前记录的反复修正修辞在大模型文本中系统性重现，过度使用被认为源于训练分布中大量推广性文本以及 RLHF 偏好自信、强调性的措辞；左到右生成只是放大器而非根因。需量化并校准模型修辞风格与人类基线的偏差。

Method: 基于 Fontanier 将 epanorthosis 归类为思想修辞，构建 Epanorthosis Index（相对人类速率的密度指标），在三种规模的指令微调模型族上测量多体裁表现；调研以轻量级 LoRA 适配器为中心的缓解技术，实验对比单行指令、监督微调适配器及缩放系数对意大利语生成中该修辞的抑制效果。

Result: 模型在演讲体裁过度使用（约 2 倍，意大利语近 3 倍，大模型更显著），在非正式问答中使用不足，在论辩、新闻、百科体裁与人类相当。单行指令可减少 1/2 至 3/4，监督微调适配器几乎完全移除，缩放系数可将减少程度调回人类速率。

Conclusion: 真正风险在于人类开始像机器一样写作；目标应是针对每个体裁将模型修辞密度校准至人类基线，而非彻底消除该修辞。

Abstract: A rhetorical figure that Cicero and Quintilian catalogued two thousand years ago reappears, systematically, in the text of large language models: epanorthosis, the self-correction of the specimen «This is not a course. It is a journey of transformation». This essay argues that the overuse is a trained disposition, driven mainly by a training distribution rich in promotional prose and by preference tuning (RLHF) that rewards confident, emphatic phrasing; the left-to-right nature of generation is an amplifier rather than the root cause. Building on evidence that models diverge from human rhetorical style, and on Fontanier's classification of epanorthosis as a figure of thought, it sets out a programme that scores the figure against genre-specific human baselines through an Epanorthosis Index (density relative to the human rate). A first measurement, on three sizes of one instruction-tuned model family, finds mis-calibration by register in both directions: the models overshoot in oratory (about twofold, near threefold in Italian, concentrated in the larger tiers) and undershoot in informal question-and-answer writing, while matching humans in argument, journalism, and encyclopedic prose. Three constructive contributions follow: a survey of mitigation techniques centred on lightweight LoRA adapters; a demonstration, in Italian, that a one-line instruction cuts the figure by half to nearly three-quarters and that a supervised-fine-tuning adapter removes it almost entirely, with a scaling coefficient that dials the reduction back onto the human rate; and the argument that the target is calibration to the human rate for each genre, not elimination. It closes on the stakes: the real risk is that we begin to write like the machines.

</details>


### [67] [DONDO: Open w2v-BERT Speech-Recognition Base Models for African Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21540)
*Paul Azunre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 DONDO 模型家族，基于 w2v-BERT 2.0 自监督编码器，面向 27 种非洲语言变体训练了 21 个单语和 5 个多语 ASR 基础模型。利用宗教文本朗读语音作为训练数据，采用两步（或三步）学习率退火微调策略，并引入轻量级语言条件机制（以 one-hot 语言标识作为前缀帧），使单一多语检查点可在推理时切换目标语言。实验显示多语模型平均 WER 10-13%，接近单语基线，模型已在 Hugging Face KhayaAI 组织以 Apache-2.0 许可开源，覆盖约 1 亿母语使用者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲语言缺乏高质量标注语音数据，现有 ASR 模型覆盖不足。宗教文本提供广泛、版权清晰、正字法一致的朗读语料，适合作为低资源语言的训练数据。同时需要一种高效的多语建模方法，既能共享参数又能在推理时灵活指定语言，降低部署成本。

Method: 1. 数据：从宗教文本收集 27 种语言的朗读语音。2. 架构：基于 w2v-BERT 2.0 编码器。3. 训练流程：先在多语混合数据上以高学习率微调共享模型，再通过学习率退火分别针对单语或多语目标继续微调（两步或三步）。4. 语言条件机制：将 one-hot 语言向量作为前缀帧拼接在声学特征前，使模型在推理时可按语言解码。

Result: 五个多语模型家族经退火微调后平均 WER 为 10%-13%，基本缩小了与单语模型的性能差距，且单一检查点覆盖多语言。模型已开源（Apache-2.0），支持商业微调。覆盖语言母语人口约 1 亿，含二语使用者更多。

Conclusion: DONDO 展示了利用宗教文本语料和学习率退火策略，结合轻量级语言条件机制，可高效构建覆盖广、性能强、许可宽松的非洲语言 ASR 基础模型，为低资源语言语音识别提供了可复现的范式。

Abstract: We present DONDO, a family of open, permissively licensed automatic speech recognition (ASR) base models for African languages, built on the w2v-BERT 2.0 self-supervised speech encoder. DONDO comprises twenty-one monolingual models and five multilingual models spanning twenty-seven language varieties across Ghana, Sierra Leone, Nigeria, Senegal, Kenya and Zimbabwe. Models are fine-tuned primarily on read speech drawn from religious texts, which offer broad, license-clear and orthographically consistent coverage for languages that otherwise lack transcribed audio. We describe a two-step (and, for one family, three-step) learning-rate-annealed fine-tuning procedure that first adapts a shared multilingual model at a high learning rate and then anneals it to recover, and in several cases surpass, strong monolingual baselines. We further describe a lightweight language-conditioning mechanism that injects a one-hot language identity as a sequence of prefix frames prepended to the acoustic features, allowing a single multilingual checkpoint to be steered to a target language at inference. Across the five multilingual families the annealed models reach average word error rates (WER) of 10-13%, closing most of the gap to monolingual models while covering many languages in a single checkpoint. All models are released on the Hugging Face KhayaAI organisation under the Apache-2.0 license (attribution only) so that others may fine-tune them freely, including for commercial use. We provide a conservative estimate that the languages covered are spoken by on the order of one hundred million first-language speakers, and by substantially more when second-language use is included.

</details>


### [68] [MedGame: Storytelling Gamification Empowered by Large Language Models for Medical Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21570)
*Qian Wu,Xinrong Zhou,Zizhan Ma,Kai Chen,Zheyao Gao,Xun Lin,Hongqiu Wu,Longfei Gou,Yixiao Liu,Ann Sin Nga Lau,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedGame框架，将静态临床病例转化为结构化、可执行的叙事游戏，采用双引擎设计：医学叙事设计器生成基于病例的临床故事线与决策节点，故事导演将其转化为依赖感知的多模态编排计划。构建了包含5000个病例的MedGame Bench基准，实验表明任务特定微调显著提升开源LLM性能并缩小与商业模型的差距；初步学生研究显示学习者认为MedGame比纯文本替代方案更具吸引力和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学教育LLM系统多聚焦于局部交互（如问答、单轮反馈），缺乏将完整临床病例组织为以决策为中心的学习轨迹的能力，亟需一种能够支持结构化、交互式、多模态临床决策训练的框架。

Method: 提出MedGame双引擎框架：(1) 医学叙事设计器：基于病例生成带有状态和决策节点的临床故事线；(2) 故事导演：将故事线转化为依赖感知的多模态编排计划，并在发布的交互平台上渲染。构建MedGame Bench（5000病例基准）及评估协议，针对医学叙事生成和故事导演任务进行任务特定微调，并开展试点学生用户研究。

Result: 任务特定微调使开源LLM在MedGame Bench上性能大幅提升，并显著缩小与商业模型的差距；试点学生研究表明，学习者主观感知MedGame比纯文本替代方案更具吸引力和实用性。

Conclusion: MedGame通过结构化叙事游戏化重塑医学临床决策训练，双引擎设计有效支撑病例到交互式学习轨迹的转换，基准构建与微调策略推动了开源模型在医学叙事生成与编排任务上的实用化，用户研究验证了其教学潜力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for medical education, but most existing systems focus on localized interactions such as question answering or single-turn feedback, rather than organizing an entire clinical case into a decision-centered learning trajectory. We introduce \textit{MedGame}, a framework that transforms static clinical cases into structured, executable storytelling games. MedGame uses a dual-engine design: a Medical Narrative Designer synthesizes case-grounded clinical storylines with states and decision nodes, while a Story Director converts them into dependency-aware multimodal orchestration plans rendered by our released interactive platform. We construct MedGame Bench, a 5,000-case benchmark and evaluation protocol for Medical Narrative Generation and Story Direction. Experiments show that task-specific fine-tuning substantially improves open-source LLMs on MedGame Bench and narrows the gap with commercial models. A pilot student study further shows that learners perceive MedGame as more engaging and useful than text-only alternatives.

</details>


### [69] [Surprisal Theory is Tautological (without Rational Grounding)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21574)
*Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证了惊讶度理论在没有额外约束的情况下是一个同义反复：任何处理难度模式都可以通过某个语言模型的惊讶度来拟合，因此该理论无法做出可证伪的预测。打破这一同义反复需要理性主义干预，即相关语言模型必须源于非经验动机的理解者模型（如记忆约束或处理目标），而不依赖于该理论试图解释的行为数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惊讶度理论认为语言单元在语境中的处理难度是其在某语言模型下惊讶度的仿射函数。作者指出这一主张在缺乏约束时陷入同义反复，且心理语言学长期隐含假设相关语言模型即训练语料的生成分布，但最新实证研究推翻了这一假设。

Method: 理论分析与逻辑论证：证明在温和技术条件下，任意非负难度度量都存在对应的语言模型使其惊讶度与之仿射相关；回顾两 decades 心理语言学研究中的隐含假设；引用近期实证证据表明更好的语料模型可能成为更差的处理难度预测器。

Result: 惊讶度理论在无额外约束下不具备可证伪性；长期以来被假设掩盖的同义反复已被实证工作揭示；打破同义反复需要引入非经验动机的理解者模型（如基于记忆约束或处理目标的理性主义模型）。

Conclusion: 惊讶度理论必须通过理性主义干预来打破同义反复：相关语言模型应源于理解者的认知架构（如记忆限制、处理目标），而非单纯拟合语料库分布。这意味着理论构建不应依赖其试图解释的行为数据，而应基于独立的认知原则。

Abstract: Surprisal theory holds that the human processing difficulty of a linguistic unit in context is an affine function of its surprisal under some language model. I argue this claim is a tautology without further constraint: for any non-negative difficulty measure over units in context, there exists a language model whose surprisal is an affine function of it under mild technical conditions. Therefore, because any pattern of difficulty is consistent with some language model, without an additional constraint on the language model, surprisal theory makes no falsifiable predictions. The tautology was long obscured by an assumption implicit in two decades of psycholinguistic work---that the relevant language model is the distribution that generated the training corpus, so that improving corpus fit improves predictions of human behavior. Recent empirical work has undermined this assumption, demonstrating that better corpus models can be worse predictors of processing difficulty. I conclude that breaking the tautology requires a rationalist intervention, i.e., the relevant language model must be derived from a non-empirically motivated model of the comprehender, which could be based on, for instance, memory constraints or processing goals, and that, thus, does not depend on the behavioral data surprisal theory is meant to explain.

</details>


### [70] [Knowledge Injection Exists in MoE? Exploring Expert-Aware Contrast Decoding in MoE for Mitigating LLMs'Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20426)
*Xinyue Fang,Zhiliang Tian,Zhen Huang,Ziyi Pan,Zhihua Wen,Xi Wang,Quntian Fang,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EAACD，一种基于MoE模型高层专家激活差异的自适应对比解码方法，通过将高层专家按置信度和一致性分为高可靠性组和低可靠性组，对比高可靠性组与各低可靠性组的预测并放大低可靠性专家的幻觉作为负向参考，有效缓解幻觉，在四个数据集上超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM幻觉缓解方法（提示工程、模型优化）要么难以改变模型内部知识，要么跨域泛化差；对比解码利用层间差异缓解幻觉，但仅探索Transformer架构（如GPT），忽略了MoE等有效框架。因MoE改变了传统Transformer架构，需研究MoE中是否存在类似层级差异，并利用其特有的专家激活模式设计新方法。

Method: 提出EAACD（专家感知自适应对比解码）。首先实证研究发现MoE共享专家无层级差异，但不同MoE高层专家激活模式在事实与非事实输出上差异明显。EAACD根据置信度和一致性将高层专家分为高可靠性组和多个低可靠性组，利用高可靠性组预测与各低可靠性组预测进行对比校准原始预测；通过注意力和掩码放大低可靠性专家的幻觉以提供更强负向参考。

Result: EAACD在四个问答数据集上均优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: EAACD利用MoE高层专家激活差异，通过自适应分组对比和幻觉放大机制，有效缓解幻觉，具有跨域泛化能力，为MoE架构下的幻觉缓解提供新思路。

Abstract: Existing LLM hallucination mitigation methods, including prompt engineering and model optimization, either hardly alter models'internal knowledge or have poor cross-domain generalization. Contrastive decoding mitigates hallucinations by using layer-wise differences in LLMs. However, prior studies only explore transformer-based models (e.g., GPT), ignoring other effective frameworks like mixture-of-experts (MoE) models. Since MoE alters the traditional transformer architecture, we conduct empirical studies to investigate whether similar layer-wise differences exist in MoEs. Our results show that they do not exist in MoE with shared experts; nevertheless, across different MoEs, higher layers exhibit distinct expert activation patterns between factual and non-factual outputs. Building on these, we propose EAACD, an expert-aware adaptive contrast decoding that uses expert differences in MoE's higher layers to mitigate hallucinations on QA tasks. EAACD splits high-layer experts into a higher-reliability group and several lower-reliability groups based on their confidence and consistency. It contrasts the higher-reliability group's prediction with each lower-reliability group's prediction to calibrate the model's original predictions. To strengthen this contrast, EAACD amplifies hallucinations from lower-reliability experts via attention and masking to provide stronger negative references. EAACD outperforms all baselines on four datasets.

</details>


### [71] [Is MoE Routing a Huffman Code? Discovering the Frequency-Diversity Law in Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20427)
*Ching-Chieh Tsao,Zhuoyi Lin,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现MoE路由机制本质上遵循霍夫曼编码原理，提出频率-多样性定律揭示模型自发充当信息论引擎，识别出Qwen3.5-35B-A3B中的冗余陷阱，并提出子集差异剪枝策略消除功能重复，证明剪枝不降低推理能力反而释放潜在霍夫曼效率，主张下一代MoE应从强制负载均衡转向最小描述长度(MDL)最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽革新了模型扩展，但其路由逻辑长期被视为黑箱。现有方法依赖启发式负载均衡，缺乏理论指导，导致在低有效稀疏度(k/E_eff)下产生功能冗余，掩盖了本应存在的霍夫曼编码效率信号。

Method: 1. 提出频率-多样性定律，分析Phi-3.5-MoE、Gemma-4-27B-A4B等SOTA模型的路由行为；2. 识别Qwen3.5-35B-A3B中负载均衡导致的功能冗余陷阱；3. 设计子集差异剪枝策略，手术式移除功能重复专家；4. 通过实验验证剪枝对推理能力的影响及霍夫曼效率的释放。

Result: 剪枝不降低推理性能，反而迫使路由逻辑坍缩为精简的高密度路径，释放了模型潜在的霍夫曼编码效率。常见token获得稀疏专家分配，稀有复杂任务（如思维链轨迹）调用高多样性专家委员会，符合信息论最优压缩原则。

Conclusion: 下一代MoE应超越强制负载均衡，转向MDL最优性：为高频信息分配更短专家路由码，为低频信息分配更长更多样化的码，将路由从启发式转变为有原则的压缩引擎。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts architectures have revolutionized scaling, yet the underlying logic of their routing remains a black box. In this paper, we uncover a fundamental governing principle: MoE routing is not merely selection, but a manifestation of Huffman Coding. We introduce the Frequency-Diversity Law, revealing that state-of-the-art models, such as Phi-3.5-MoE and Gemma-4-27B-A4B, spontaneously act as information-theoretic engines. These models allocate sparse expert resources for common tokens while invoking high-diversity expert committees for rare, complex tasks found in chain-of-thought trajectories. However, we identify a critical redundancy trap in Qwen3.5-35B-A3B: when effective sparsity (k/E_eff) is sufficiently low, load-balancing inadvertently imposes functional redundancy, masking the underlying Huffman efficiency signal. To bridge this gap, we propose Subset Difference Pruning, a surgical strategy to eliminate functional duplicates. We demonstrate that pruning does not degrade reasoning; instead, it unleashes the model's latent Huffman efficiency, forcing the logic to collapse into streamlined, high-density paths. Our findings suggest that the next generation of MoEs should move beyond forced load-balancing toward Minimum Description Length (MDL) optimality, assigning shorter expert-routing codes to high-frequency information and longer, more diverse codes to low-frequency information, thereby transforming routing from a heuristic into a principled compression engine.

</details>


### [72] [Moir: Let the Model Direct Its Own Story for Robust Cross-Domain Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20433)
*Jea Kwon,Jiwon Kim,Dong-kyum Kim,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Moir 方法，通过从模型自身的解码分布采样来估计保存协方差，解决了知识编辑中核心能力（数学和程序推理）退化的问题。Moir 无需外部数据，可作为即插即用组件集成到任何基于协方差的编辑器中，在多个模型和编辑器上显著提升了编辑后的能力保持率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于协方差的知识编辑方法（如 MEMIT、AlphaEdit）依赖静态外部语料库（如 Wikipedia）来估计保存协方差，但这些语料库无法捕捉模型经过 SFT 和 DPO 等后训练后的操作性分布，导致编辑过程中数学和程序推理等核心能力严重退化，而百科知识回忆却保持完好。这种不对称退化源于参考语料库分布与模型实际操作分布的不匹配。

Method: 提出 Moir 方法：从模型自身的解码分布中采样来直接估计保存协方差 C。使用单个随机词汇 token 作为种子启动生成，绕过指令跟随模板对采样输出的主导，暴露模型内化的更广泛子空间。Moir 无需外部数据，可直接作为即插即用组件集成到任何基于协方差的编辑器中。

Result: 在 OLMo-2、Llama-3.1 和 Qwen-3 (7-8B) 模型上，结合 MEMIT 和 AlphaEdit 编辑器，在批量和顺序编辑模式下，Moir 一致地在最脆弱的领域扩展了保持能力。最显著的是在 Qwen3-8B 上进行 20,000 次 AlphaEdit 批量编辑后，GSM8K 准确率保持 79.9%，而 Wikipedia 基线仅 10.9%。

Conclusion: 将保存分布与模型的操作性分布对齐是无损知识编辑的关键因素，对于部署系统而言，模型自身可能是获取该分布的最可行来源。Moir 提供了一种实用且有效的解决方案，无需访问预训练或后训练语料库即可显著缓解编辑过程中的能力退化。

Abstract: While language models remain frozen at their training state, the world evolves continuously. Knowledge editing has emerged as a key alternative to full retraining, but its deployment is bottlenecked by the erosion of core capabilities: mathematical and programmatic reasoning collapse while encyclopedic recall remains intact. We trace this asymmetric degradation to a distributional mismatch. Covariance-based editors preserve only the subspaces spanned by their reference corpus, but fail to capture the operative distribution shaped by post-training such as SFT and DPO. Static external corpora, including Wikipedia and even the original pretraining mixture, cannot recover this shifted manifold. We propose Moir, which estimates the preservation covariance $C$ directly from the model itself by sampling from its own decoding distribution. Seeding generation with a single random vocabulary token bypasses the instruction-following templates that otherwise dominate sampled outputs, exposing the broader subspaces the model has internalized. Moir requires no external data and serves as a drop-in component for any covariance-based editor, a practical advantage given that the pre- and post-training corpora of most modern LLMs are not publicly accessible. Across OLMo-2, Llama-3.1, and Qwen-3 (7-8B), under both MEMIT and AlphaEdit and in batch and sequential regimes, Moir consistently extends preservation in the most vulnerable domains, most strikingly on Qwen3-8B after 20,000 AlphaEdit batch edits, it retains 79.9% GSM8K accuracy compared to 10.9% with the Wikipedia baseline. These results suggest that aligning the preservation distribution with the model's operative distribution is a key factor in non-destructive editing, and that the model itself may be the most accessible source of that distribution for deployed systems.

</details>


### [73] [Break Through the Compression Bottleneck: From Theory to Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20434)
*Xiusheng Huang,Lu Wang,Yequan Wang,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文证明了低秩分解与量化在大语言模型压缩中并非正交，二者结合会导致显著性能下降，并提出对角粘合法（DAM）有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型参数规模增长，需要有效压缩以减少计算和内存开销。现有压缩方法在高压缩比下性能急剧下降。低秩分解和量化是两种主流压缩技术，人们普遍认为二者正交，结合不会引入额外误差。但这一假设缺乏理论验证，且实际结合时性能退化严重，亟需理论分析和改进方法。

Method: 1. 数学证明低秩分解与量化的非正交性；2. 在大语言模型上进行实验验证；3. 提出对角粘合法（DAM），通过对角矩阵粘合低秩分解与量化，减少组合误差。

Result: 实验表明低秩分解与量化确实非正交，直接结合会导致显著性能下降。DAM 方法能有效结合两种压缩技术，缓解性能损失，在保持高压缩比的同时维持模型精度。

Conclusion: 本文首次从理论和实验上揭示了低秩分解与量化的非正交本质，提出的 DAM 方法为突破模型压缩瓶颈提供了可行路径，为后续压缩研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: As the parameter size of language models continues to grow, effective model compression is required to reduce their computational and memory overhead. Existing compression methods suffer from bottleneck issues: when the compression ratio is increased, performance degrades significantly. Low-rank decomposition and quantization are two prominent compression methods that have been proven to significantly reduce the computational and memory requirements of Large Language Models (LLMs) while maintaining model accuracy. Evidently, combining these two methods will break through the existing compression bottleneck. However, how these two methods interact when combined remains a critical question for developers, as many assume they are orthogonal, meaning their combination would not introduce additional errors beyond those independently introduced by each method. This paper provides the first mathematical proof that low-rank decomposition and quantization are non-orthogonal. We validate these findings through a series of experiments on large language models. Our results demonstrate that these methods are non-orthogonal, and their combination leads to significant performance degradation. Importantly, we propose a novel approach Diagonal Adhesive Method (DAM), which can effectively combine the two methods and mitigate the performance loss. Our research provides deep insights into model compression and lays a solid theoretical and experimental foundation for future related studies.

</details>

### [74] [LO-FAR: A Cost-Aware Local Filter for Sparse Feature Ranking in Industrial Ad Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20873)
*Egemen Erbayat,Luis Duque,Sohini Roychowdhury,Mohammad Amin,Srihari Reddy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 LO-FAR，一种仅需 CPU、与模型无关的稀疏 ID-list 特征排序工作流，利用轻量级局部估计器在约两小时内完成 475 个特征的排序，且在 CTR 和 CVR 任务上保持了与交互感知方法相当的归一化熵增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界广告推荐模型严重依赖高基数、稀疏的 ID-list 特征，这些特征占据存储、训练和服务成本的大头，且随着流量和下游模型演进需周期性重新评估。现有基于置换、随机门控等方法需要 GPU 重训练循环，计算预算和迭代周期受限，亟需一种低成本、快速周转的特征排序方案。

Method: LO-FAR 采用纯 CPU、模型无关的流程：对每个候选特征单独使用轻量级局部估计器（如梯度提升树或逻辑回归）在保留的验证集上评估其独立预测信号，据此对所有特征进行排序，完全避免了 GPU 密集型的重训练或交互感知搜索。

Result: 在超过 100 万条日志交互和 475 个稀疏 ID-list 特征的生产数据集上，LO-FAR 约两 CPU 小时完成排序。在保留 100–400 个特征的不同预算下，其在 CTR 和 CVR 任务上的归一化熵增益与基于 Shuffle 的重要性、Binary Stochastic Neurons 以及基于覆盖率的启发式方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 当计算成本和周转时间成为硬性约束时，基于局部独立信号的简单过滤器（LO-FAR）是一种可落地的生产选择，能以极低开销实现与复杂交互感知方法相当的特征剪枝效果。

Abstract: Industrial ad recommendation models rely heavily on sparse, high-cardinality ID-list features that encode user histories and contextual identifiers. Each is backed by a dedicated embedding table, so these features dominate storage, training, and serving cost and must be revisited as traffic and downstream models evolve. Therefore, sparse feature ranking is not just an offline modeling problem but also a recurrent systems decision limited by compute budgets and iteration cadence. We present Localized Feature Ranking (LO-FAR), a CPU-only, model-agnostic workflow that ranks each candidate feature from its stand-alone held-out predictive signal using lightweight local estimators rather than the GPU-bound retraining loops of permutation- and stochastic-gate-based methods. On a production dataset of more than one million logged interactions and 475 sparse ID-list features, LO-FAR completes ranking in approximately two CPU-hours and preserves downstream Normalized Entropy gains on CTR and CVR tasks that are competitive with shuffle-based importance, Binary Stochastic Neurons, and a coverage-based heuristic across budgets of 100--400 retained features. The contribution is a deployable workflow showing that, when cost and turnaround constraints are binding, a simple local filter can be a practical production choice over heavier interaction-aware alternatives.

</details>


### [75] [Bridging the Structural Gap: Adapting Autoregressive Generation for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21028)
*Junchao Zeng,Junzhang Zhu,Junyang Chen,Yudong Li,Wei Liu,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 BARGE 模型，针对生成式推荐（GR）中扁平化多 token ID 破坏物品级结构以及训练-推理不一致导致的语义漂移两大结构性缺口，引入物品感知注意力（ICA）恢复编码端的物品级结构，并通过层级路径重排序（HPR）与双路径解码（DPD）从两个互补角度抑制解码端的语义漂移。在公开基准、大规模离线测试及腾讯平台在线 A/B 实验中均取得显著提升，验证了其工业级应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐（GR）将物品编码为层级语义 ID 并逐 token 预测，但现有方法存在两个核心结构性缺口：1) 将多 token ID 展平为单一序列破坏了物品级的结构信息；2) 训练与推理时在层级码本上的不一致导致语义漂移。这两个问题限制了 GR 在序列推荐任务上的表现，亟需解决。

Method: 提出 BARGE 框架，包含三大核心组件：1) Item Context-Aware Attention (ICA)：在编码阶段引入物品上下文感知注意力机制，恢复被扁平化破坏的物品级结构；2) Hierarchical Path Reranking (HPR)：在解码阶段通过层级路径重排序，从码本层面校正语义漂移；3) Dual-Path Decoding (DPD)：采用双路径解码策略，从预测路径层面进一步抑制语义漂移。三者协同作用，分别从编码与解码两端弥补结构性缺口。

Result: 在多个公开基准数据集和大规模离线测试中，BARGE 均显著优于现有生成式推荐基线。腾讯平台在线 A/B 实验显示：点击率（CTR）提升 0.60%，点击独立访客（Click UV）提升 1.34%，总阅读时长提升 1.70%，充分验证了模型在工业级推荐系统中的实际效果。

Conclusion: BARGE 通过 ICA 恢复物品级结构、HPR 与 DPD 双重抑制语义漂移，有效弥合了生成式推荐与推荐任务之间的结构性鸿沟。大量离线实验与在线 A/B 测试均证明其优越性，为工业界大规模生成式推荐系统提供了可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) has emerged as a new paradigm for sequential recommendation, in which a representative line of work encodes items into hierarchical semantic IDs via residual quantization and predicts the IDs token by token. However, this generative formulation still exhibits structural gaps with respect to the recommendation task: flattening multi-token IDs into a single sequence destroys item-level structure, and the inconsistency between training and inference over a hierarchical codebook gives rise to semantic drift. To bridge these two gaps, we propose BARGE, which employs Item Context-Aware Attention (ICA) to restore item-level structure during encoding, and Hierarchical Path Reranking (HPR) together with Dual-Path Decoding (DPD) to suppress semantic drift from two complementary angles during decoding. Extensive experiments and analytical studies on public benchmarks and a large-scale offline test demonstrate that BARGE achieves superior recommendation performance. An online A/B test on a Tencent platform yields improvements of 0.60% in click-through rate, 1.34% in click unique visitors, and 1.70% in total reading time, confirming the practical value of BARGE in industrial-scale recommendation.

</details>


### [76] [Diffusion Language Model for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21519)
*Chengyi Liu,Yongqi Zhou,Junwei Pan,Zhixiang Feng,Chengguo Yin,Haijie Gu,Jie Jiang,Yinghao Liu,Yujuan Ding,Qing Li,Wenqi Fan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DLMRec，一种用于推荐系统的离散扩散语言模型，通过协作感知随机分词器、课程驱动训练策略和稳定性感知投票机制三大核心组件，解决自回归生成在推荐任务中的局限性，为生成式推荐提供了有竞争力的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的推荐系统多采用自回归范式，但该范式存在两大缺陷：一是下一词预测目标过分强调序列顺序，忽视了用户偏好背后的结构性物品间依赖；二是前缀约束生成限制了双向上下文，导致左向右解码时早期错误无法纠正且不断累积。

Method: DLMRec引入三大关键组件：1) 协作感知随机分词器，将多跳协作信号编码为兼容扩散建模的表达性离散token；2) 课程驱动训练策略，通过渐进式的物品级和token级学习，使去噪过程与偏好恢复对齐；3) 稳定性感知投票机制，聚合迭代预测以提升生成的一致性和鲁棒性。

Result: 论文提出了DLMRec框架，并通过三大创新组件将离散扩散语言建模与推荐任务有效结合，为生成式推荐提供了区别于自回归范式的新选择。

Conclusion: DLMRec作为一种针对推荐定制的离散扩散语言模型，克服了自回归生成在序列建模和错误累积上的不足，展示了扩散语言模型在生成式推荐中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-empowered recommender systems have emerged as a promising paradigm for generative recommendation, leveraging their strong semantic reasoning and generative capacity to model complex, diverse user preferences. However, most existing approaches rely on an autoregressive paradigm that is suboptimal for recommendation. The next-token objective emphasizes sequential order rather than the structural inter-item dependencies underlying user preferences. In addition, prefix-constrained generation restricts bidirectional context and commits to left-to-right decoding, causing early errors to accumulate without correction. Inspired by the success of diffusion language models, we propose \textbf{DLMRec}, a discrete diffusion language model tailored for recommendation that offers a compelling alternative to autoregressive generation. Specifically, DLMRec introduces three key components to bridge diffusion language modeling with recommendation. First, a collaborative-aware stochastic tokenizer encodes multi-hop collaborative signals into expressive discrete tokens compatible with diffusion modeling. Second, a curriculum-driven training strategy aligns the denoising process with preference recovery through progressive item- and token-level learning. Third, a stability-aware voting mechanism aggregates iterative predictions to improve generation consistency and robustness.

</details>


### [77] [Probabilistic Residual Learning for Online Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20863)
*Wenyuan Wang,Yusong Zhao,Zihao Xu,Hengyi Wang,Qi Xu,Zhigang Hua,Yan Xie,Yi Wang,Zihao Zhao,Bo Long,Chengzhi Mao,Shuang Yang,Hengguan Huang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出概率残差学习（PRL），一种因果贝叶斯推荐模型，通过建模真实标签与基础预测之间的残差，实现对现有深度学习推荐系统的即插即用式改进。PRL 结合用户概率聚类、领域级混杂因子建模与 do-calculus 聚合，在多个基础模型上提升推荐性能并自动发现有意义的用户群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于深度学习的推荐系统通常采用稠密编码器学习用户和物品表示，导致模型呈现黑盒特性且计算复杂度高，难以系统性地增强推荐能力。因此需要一种能够针对性修正现有预测、兼容多种基础模型且具备可解释性的方法。

Method: 提出 Probabilistic Residual Learning (PRL) 模型：(1) 以概率方式对用户分组，实现局部化残差建模；(2) 引入领域级混杂因子，捕捉影响用户和物品表示的共同原因；(3) 利用 do-calculus 在混杂因子上聚合簇特定的残差预测，得到因果干预下的修正预测。PRL 可作为即插即用模块接入任意基础深度学习推荐器。

Result: 实验表明，PRL 兼容多种基础深度学习推荐系统，显著提升推荐性能（如 Recall、NDCG 等指标），同时自动发现具有语义意义的用户簇，验证了因果建模与残差学习结合的有效性。

Conclusion: PRL 通过因果贝叶斯框架对残差进行建模，解决了黑盒深度推荐模型难以系统改进的问题，提供了一种可解释、可插拔且性能优越的推荐增强方案。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems are typically based on deep learning (DL) models, where a dense encoder learns representations of users and items. As a result, these systems often suffer from the black-box nature and computational complexity of the underlying models, making it difficult to systematically enhance their recommendation capabilities. To address this problem, we propose Probabilistic Residual Learning (PRL), a causal Bayesian recommendation model that models the residual between ground-truth and base predictions, enabling targeted refinement of existing systems. Specifically, PRL (1) probabilistically groups users for localized residual modeling, (2) models domain-level confounders that influence user and item representations, and (3) aggregates cluster-specific residual predictions over the confounders using do-calculus. Experiments demonstrate that our plug-and-play PRL is compatible with various base deep learning recommender systems, improving their performance while automatically discovering meaningful user clusters.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [78] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DC-Leap，一种无需训练的加速扩散大语言模型（dLLM）推理的框架，通过动态连续验证策略和草稿引导解码机制，有效缓解联合概率依赖误差（JPDE），在保持生成质量的同时实现高达105倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前dLLM的并行解码受限于过于保守的置信度阈值，这是由联合概率依赖误差（JPDE）导致的，造成冗余去噪迭代和次优推理速度。

Method: DC-Leap包含两大核心组件：1) 动态连续验证策略，将严格有序的因果约束集成到并行解码中，通过逐步验证token依赖关系消除JPDE；2) 草稿引导解码机制，利用草稿跨越多个token向前跳跃扩展上下文，提供前瞻上下文并保留双向注意力的结构优势。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，DC-Leap在MBPP长序列生成任务上实现高达53.19倍加速，结合KV-Cache时最高达105.02倍加速，且生成质量相当。

Conclusion: DC-Leap通过创新的验证策略和草稿机制，在无需训练的情况下显著加速dLLM推理，解决了JPDE导致的效率瓶颈，为实际部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [79] [InferenceBench: A Benchmark for Open-Ended LLM Inference Optimization by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20468)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Ben Rank,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 InferenceBench 基准，要求 AI 智能体在 2 小时内于单张 H100 GPU 上部署并优化 OpenAI 兼容推理服务器。实验显示 15 个前沿智能体配置均能大幅超越朴素 PyTorch 基线（最高 8.08×），常能匹配或超过 vLLM 默认设置（4.05×），但仍落后于同等时间预算下的简单超参数搜索（最高 11.53×）。定性分析揭示智能体倾向于收敛单一推理框架，尝试配置极少，多数预算花在重复测量、修复与超参数调优而非探索多样策略，瓶颈在于提出多样化配置、系统评估并提交最优解的能力，而非领域知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准多针对固定工作流或狭窄动作空间，甚至名义上开放的任务也常能通过检索已知配方并微调超参数解决，难以区分真正的优化能力与记忆方案。作者希望构建一个开放式的 AI 工程任务——LLM 推理加速，迫使智能体在真实工程环境中展示系统性探索与决策能力。

Method: 设计 InferenceBench：给定目标 LLM、单张 H100、2 小时墙钟时间，包含四个优化场景（prefill 延迟、decode 延迟、并发吞吐、三者平衡）。对 15 个前沿智能体配置进行评测，对比朴素 PyTorch 基线、vLLM 默认设置、同等时间预算下的简单超参数搜索。并对智能体轨迹进行定性分析，统计尝试的配置多样性、框架收敛情况及时间分配。

Result: 智能体最高比朴素基线快 8.08×，常匹配/超越 vLLM 默认（4.05×），但均低于简单超参数搜索（最高 11.53×）。轨迹显示智能体虽枚举多种优化技术，却高度收敛于单一推理框架，仅测试极少数不同配置，剩余预算主要用于重复测量、修复与超参数微调。

Conclusion: InferenceBench 暴露当前智能体在开放式 AI 工程任务中的核心短板：缺乏提出多样化配置、系统评估并提交最优解的能力，而非领域知识不足。记忆式方案仅能带来有限提升，真正的优化需要更强的探索与决策机制。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to automate research and development tasks, yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate them on prescribed workflows or narrow action spaces. Even nominally open-ended tasks can often be solved by retrieving a well-known recipe and tuning a few hyperparameters, making it unclear whether strong results reflect genuine optimization or memorized solutions. We introduce InferenceBench, where an agent must deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server and optimize the speed of LLM inference. Each agent receives a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a wall-clock time budget of two hours. Three optimization scenarios isolate distinct bottlenecks of inference (prefill latency, decode latency, and concurrent request throughput) and a fourth balances all three at the same time. Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents reliably improve over a naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and often match or exceed serving engines with default settings ($4.05\times$ for vLLM), but still fall below a simple hyperparameter search under the same time budget (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis of agent trajectories shows that although agents enumerate many relevant optimization techniques, they overwhelmingly converge on a single inference framework. They test only a few distinct configurations and spend the remaining budget re-measuring, repairing, or optimizing hyperparameters rather than exploring substantially different strategies. This suggests the bottleneck is not domain knowledge, but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best identified solution. Overall, InferenceBench reflects the ability of agents to operate in an open-ended AI engineering setting, where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

</details>


### [80] [DecodeShare: Tracing the Shared Subspace of LLM Decode-Time Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20469)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Zhixu Du,Yuzhe Fu,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 DecodeShare 协议，发现大语言模型解码阶段隐藏状态中存在一个跨任务共享的低维子空间，证明其因果作用，并展示该子空间在激活引导中的实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在 KV 缓存推理下，不清楚解码时刻是否使用任务通用结构（而非预填充阶段）。需要识别解码时共享的低维子空间并验证其因果作用。

Method: 提出 DecodeShare 协议：识别解码时隐藏状态中跨任务一致共享的低维子空间，通过仅在解码阶段移除该子空间来测试其因果作用；对比扰动共享子空间、预填充推导子空间和随机子空间的效果；研究激活引导中共享子空间与引导方向的重叠关系。

Result: 扰动发现的共享子空间比扰动预填充推导或随机子空间更严重地降低决策性能；解码共享子空间与常见引导方向重叠；投影移除共享子空间可分离两组件功能角色；解码时评估引导向量比预填充代理更可靠。

Conclusion: 尽管紧凑，共享子空间可作为解码时的高杠杆因果通道，为理解 LLM 解码机制和激活引导提供新见解。
